Run 39114949128 (cap 128 on the first bundle image a8ed4592) scored
7999.37 (5051 requests, 98.3% good, steady ITL 14.0 / p99 16.3 ms, zero
ITL failures, 84 TTFT failures all in the start burst) against 7567.56
for 39094011392 and 7082.82 for the same image at cap 136. Every Decode
MegaMoE call ran SwapAB at 194.5 us (A1: 212.6 us), FA3 decode and the
dense GEMMs returned to 50.3 / 17.2 us, Decode GPUs stayed 98.3% busy
with async admission. Decode averaged 116 running requests under the
128 cap, so Prefill supply now bounds throughput.

This run serves the image built by 39115728016 (913b88b, the parent of
this commit) with the same runtime configuration as 39114949128 (cap 128,
graph bs up to 32, c192, all four runtime switches). It adds the flat
NVLink barriers (all geometries), the restored fused add-RMSNorm + QKV
quant and SwapAB selection up to 144 tokens. With the cap at 128 the
SwapAB extension is inactive in serving (it only matters above 128
tokens per rank); the expected gain is in Prefill (barrier removal in the
Prefill MegaMoE, norm + quant ~0.5%) and a few us per Decode call, about
+1-2% at most. The pre-serving gate exercises the new barrier protocol,
including mixed geometries and graph replays, before the server starts.
